2026-11-01T14:42:56.3168805Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 20 again after making two capture tests independent of the Python version and thread order

Run 41315801020 for c798d7b stopped in validation before any build or
GPU use. Two round-20 tests failed under CI's interpreter, which is the
project's CPython 3.13 (uv run --project qwen-experiment-metrics), while
they had passed under the no-project 3.12 used locally:
- test_capture_does_nothing_during_measurement_even_when_configured
  puts a fake torch into sys.modules whose every attribute access fails
  as "CUDA during measurement". CPython 3.13 probes __spec__ of a
  sys.modules entry during import, so the fake failed before the code
  under test ran. Dunder lookups now raise AttributeError; any other
  attribute is still forbidden.
- test_capture_controller_checks_all_dp_peers_before_paired_dispatch
  compared consecutive /generate calls pairwise, but the capture
  controller sends them through a thread pool, so their recorded order
  is not fixed. The test now checks the multiset: three distinct bodies,
  each sent exactly twice, to a Prefill and a Decode worker.

Production code is unchanged. Everything else is as described in
c798d7b: P is revalidated and served, candidate L (load-balanced FC1
tile order, bit-exact by design) is gated on all 12 GPUs and three
routings with a 10 us per layer margin, the uninstrumented MegaMoE is
timed in the same resident process, and serving routing is captured
after the official measurement. The image pin stays at main's 5a3df8c4;
this builds a fresh image.

Validation: the full suite exits 0 both CI-style (uv run --project
qwen-experiment-metrics with numpy and pytest, CPython 3.13.12) and
under the no-project Python 3.12 run used before.
